RBI PDFs for the Loan Guarantor corpus

Click each link and save the PDF into data/raw/pdf/ without renaming it. Then run python -m src.ingest.parse and python -m src.index.hybrid build. Any document without a PDF keeps its web page text. Re-run python -m src.ingest.pdf_list to refresh the ticks.
